- **release**: Cut an Actana Control release. A release is a promotion of a train, not a tag push — promote.yml is dispatched against beta/x.y.z, its first job waits on a human, and the digest check, the fast-forward of main, the tag, release.yml and the Docker Hub images are all downstream of that approval. The procedure lives in docs/ci-cd.md § "Cutting a release"; this skill is the pointer to it, the two rules that are expensive to learn by doing, the preconditions no workflow checks for you, and the places ADR 0023 and the workflows do not agree. ([actana/control](https://github.com/actana/control/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/release) · ★5)
